<%# JS — connecte la tranche sélectionnée à l'affichage Grand Nancy %>
<script>
(function() {
  var amountsEl = document.getElementById('aide-grand-nancy-amount');
  if (!amountsEl) return;
  var amounts = JSON.parse(amountsEl.dataset.amounts || '[0,0,0,0]');

  function updateGrandNancy(profil) {
    var amount = amounts[profil] || 0;
    amountsEl.textContent = amount > 0
      ? amount.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'
      : '—';
    // Mettre en surbrillance la bonne tranche
    document.querySelectorAll('.gn-bracket').forEach(function(el) {
      var isActive = parseInt(el.dataset.idx) === profil;
      el.style.borderColor   = isActive ? '#059669' : 'transparent';
      el.style.background    = isActive ? '#ecfdf5' : 'white';
    });
  }

  // Surcharger setProfil pour qu'il mette aussi à jour le bloc Grand Nancy
  var _originalSetProfil = window.setProfil;
  window.setProfil = function(p) {
    if (_originalSetProfil) _originalSetProfil(p);
    updateGrandNancy(p);
  };

  // Init : si un profil est déjà actif au chargement
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    if (typeof currentProfil !== 'undefined' && currentProfil >= 0) {
      updateGrandNancy(currentProfil);
    }
  });
})();
</script>

<script>
function setOccupation(isVacant) {
  document.getElementById('vacant-field').value = isVacant;

  const labelOccupe = document.getElementById('label-occupe');
  const labelVacant = document.getElementById('label-vacant');
  const vacantQ     = document.getElementById('vacant-questions');
  const hint        = document.getElementById('description-hint');
  const desc        = document.getElementById('description-field');

  if (isVacant) {
    labelVacant.style.border = '2px solid #059669';
    labelVacant.style.background = '#f0fdf4';
    labelOccupe.style.border = '2px solid #e5e7eb';
    labelOccupe.style.background = 'white';
    vacantQ.style.display = 'block';
    hint.textContent = 'Ajoutez tout ce qui peut aider Lotys à mieux comprendre la situation de ce logement.';
    desc.placeholder = 'Ex : appartement hérité de ma mère en 2022, en mauvais état. Trois héritiers, décision à prendre ensemble. Personne ne souhaite le conserver.';
  } else {
    labelOccupe.style.border = '2px solid #059669';
    labelOccupe.style.background = '#f0fdf4';
    labelVacant.style.border = '2px solid #e5e7eb';
    labelVacant.style.background = 'white';
    vacantQ.style.display = 'none';
    hint.textContent = 'Un contexte que vous souhaitez partager avec Lotys.';
    desc.placeholder = "Ex : j'ai hérité de ce bien, je souhaite évaluer s'il vaut mieux rénover ou vendre. Les relations en copropriété sont tendues.";
  }
}

// Pré-sélectionner "vacant" si source brigade
<% if is_brigade %>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => setOccupation(true));
<% end %>

function showFileList(input) {
  const list = document.getElementById('file-list');
  list.innerHTML = '<p class="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">Fichiers sélectionnés (' + input.files.length + ')</p>';
  list.classList.remove('hidden');
  Array.from(input.files).forEach(file => {
    const ext  = file.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
    const icon = ['jpg','jpeg','png'].includes(ext) ? '📸' : '📄';
    const size = (file.size / 1024).toFixed(0) + ' Ko';
    list.innerHTML += `<div class="flex items-center justify-between bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm">
      <span class="text-gray-700">${icon} ${file.name}</span>
      <span class="text-gray-400 text-xs">${size}</span>
    </div>`;
  });
}

function setIncomeBracket(val, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('[onclick^="setIncomeBracket"]').forEach(function(l) {
    l.style.borderColor = '#e5e7eb';
    l.style.background  = 'white';
  });
  el.style.borderColor = '#059669';
  el.style.background  = '#f0fdf4';
}
</script>
<script>
function checkPhotoLimit(input) {
  const warning = document.getElementById("photo-limit-warning");
  if (input.files.length > 10) {
    warning.classList.remove("hidden");
    input.value = "";
  } else {
    warning.classList.add("hidden");
  }
}

document.querySelector("form").addEventListener("submit", function() {
  window.scrollTo(0,0); document.getElementById("loading-overlay").style.display = "flex";
  document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
});
</script>

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:20px;" id="offer-type-cards">
          <% [
            [:travaux,    "🔨", "Devis rénovation / travaux",               "Isolation, chauffage, menuiseries — vous proposez une intervention chiffrée."],

          ].each do |val, emoji, titre, desc| %>
            <label id="card-<%= val %>" onclick="selectType('<%= val %>')"
              style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;padding:14px 16px;border:1.5px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;transition:border-color .15s;">
              <%= f.radio_button :offer_type, val, style:"display:none;", id:"radio-#{val}", onchange:"selectType('#{val}')" %>
              <span style="font-size:1.3rem;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;"><%= emoji %></span>
              <div>
                <p class="bod" style="font-weight:700;font-size:.9rem;margin-bottom:2px;"><%= titre %></p>
                <p class="lbl" style="text-transform:none;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:0;color:#6b7280;"><%= desc %></p>
              </div>
            </label>
          <% end %>
        </div>

        <%# Montant (optionnel) %>
        <div style="margin-bottom:16px;">
          <label class="lbl" style="display:block;margin-bottom:6px;">Montant estimé (optionnel)</label>
          <%= f.number_field :amount, placeholder: "ex. 12000", style:"width:100%;padding:10px 14px;border:1.5px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;font-size:.9rem;color:#111;" %>
        </div>

        <%# Message %>
        <div style="margin-bottom:20px;">
          <label class="lbl" style="display:block;margin-bottom:6px;">Votre message</label>
          <p style="font-size:.78rem;color:#9ca3af;margin-bottom:6px;">Décrivez votre proposition clairement.</p>
          <%= f.text_area :description, rows: 5, placeholder: "Décrivez votre proposition...",
              style:"width:100%;padding:10px 14px;border:1.5px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;font-size:.9rem;color:#111;resize:vertical;" %>
        </div>

        <%= f.submit "Envoyer ma proposition →",
            style:"display:block;width:100%;background:#059669;color:#fff;font-size:1rem;font-weight:800;padding:16px;border-radius:14px;border:none;cursor:pointer;" %>
        <p class="lbl" style="text-align:center;margin-top:10px;color:#9ca3af;">Votre identité n'est pas visible du propriétaire avant qu'il accepte votre offre.</p>

      <% end %>

    <% else %>
      <p class="lbl" style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:10px;">Créez un compte prestataire pour répondre à ce dossier</p>
      <%= link_to "S'inscrire et faire une proposition →", new_user_registration_path,
          style:"display:block;text-align:center;background:#1d4ed8;color:#fff;font-size:1rem;font-weight:800;padding:16px;border-radius:14px;text-decoration:none;" %>
    <% end %>

  </div>
</div>

<script>
function selectType(val) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#offer-type-cards label').forEach(function(card) {
    card.style.borderColor = '#e5e7eb';
    card.style.background  = '#fff';
  });
  var card = document.getElementById('card-' + val);
  if (card) { card.style.borderColor = '#059669'; card.style.background = '#f0fdf4'; }
  var radio = document.getElementById('radio-' + val);
  if (radio) radio.checked = true;
}
// Pré-sélectionner si déjà coché (retour d'erreur)
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  var checked = document.querySelector('#offer-type-cards input[type=radio]:checked');
  if (checked) selectType(checked.value);
});
</script>

===== app/views/properties/show.html.erb =====
<%# show.html.erb — vue unifiée propriétaire + prestataire %>

<% parsed    = @property.analysis&.content.present? ? (JSON.parse(@property.analysis.content) rescue nil) : nil %>
<% v         = parsed&.[]("valeur") %>
<% e         = parsed&.[]("energie") %>
<% i_        = parsed&.[]("idees") %>
<% is_owner  = user_signed_in? && current_user == @property.user %>

<% dpe_all    = %w[A B C D E F G] %>
<% dpe_colors = {"A"=>"#16a34a","B"=>"#4ade80","C"=>"#a3e635","D"=>"#ca8a04","E"=>"#fb923c","F"=>"#ef4444","G"=>"#991b1b"} %>
<% dpe_labels = {"A"=>"Très performant","B"=>"Performant","C"=>"Assez performant","D"=>"Peu performant","E"=>"Énergivore","F"=>"Très énergivore","G"=>"Passoire thermique"} %>
<% dpe_cur    = (@property.dpe_class || e&.[]("dpe_estime"))&.upcase %>
<% dpe_cib    = (e&.[]("dpe_cible"))&.upcase %>
<% travaux    = (e&.[]("travaux") || []).sort_by{|t| t["priorite"].to_i} %>
<% cur_idx    = dpe_all.index(dpe_cur) || 5 %>
<% tgt_idx    = dpe_cib ? [dpe_all.index(dpe_cib) || (cur_idx-2), 0].max : [cur_idx-2, 0].max %>

<% mpr_taux = case @property.income_bracket&.to_s
     when "tres_modeste" then 0.70; when "modeste" then 0.50
     when "intermediaire" then 0.35; else 0.15; end %>
<% budget_max  = travaux.sum{|t| t["cout_max"].to_i} %>
<% budget_min  = travaux.sum{|t| t["cout_min"].to_i} %>
<% mpr_montant = budget_max > 0 ? [( budget_max * mpr_taux).round(-2), 20000].min : 0 %>
<% cee_montant = budget_max > 0 ? [(budget_max * 0.08).round(-2), 4000].min : 0 %>
<% sauts       = dpe_cur && dpe_cib ? (dpe_all.index(dpe_cur).to_i - dpe_all.index(dpe_cib).to_i) : 2 %>
<% eco_ptz     = sauts >= 3 ? 50000 : 30000 %>
<% total_aides = mpr_montant + cee_montant %>
<% reste       = [budget_max - total_aides, 0].max %>

  .lbl { font-size:.68rem; font-weight:700; color:#9ca3af;
         text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.07em; }
  .bod { font-size:.88rem; color:#374151; line-height:1.6; }

            text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.09em; color:#fff; }

  .sv-prof.on { border-color:#5b21b6; background:#faf5ff; color:#5b21b6; }

  <script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
  <script>
  function initPropMap(){
    var el=document.getElementById('prop-map');
    if(!el||el._leaflet_id)return;
    var coords={"3 impasse du canal":[48.685,6.189]};
    var addr="<%= @property.address %>";
    var ll=coords[addr]||[48.6921,6.1844];
    var col={"A":"#16a34a","B":"#22c55e","C":"#84cc16","D":"#ca8a04","E":"#f97316","F":"#dc2626","G":"#991b1b"};
    var c=col["<%= @property.dpe_class&.upcase || 'F' %>"]||"#6b7280";
    var map=L.map(el,{zoomControl:false,dragging:false,scrollWheelZoom:false,attributionControl:false}).setView(ll,15);
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',{maxZoom:19}).addTo(map);
    var icon=L.divIcon({html:'<div style="width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:50%;background:'+c+';display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:.75rem;font-weight:900;color:#fff;border:2px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.3);">'+"<%= @property.dpe_class&.upcase || '?' %>"+'</div>',className:'',iconSize:[28,28],iconAnchor:[14,14]});
    L.marker(ll,{icon:icon}).addTo(map);
  }
  document.addEventListener('turbo:load',initPropMap);
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initPropMap);
  </script>

<script>
var dpe_all=['A','B','C','D','E','F','G'];
var C_DPE={A:'#16a34a',B:'#4ade80',C:'#a3e635',D:'#ca8a04',E:'#fb923c',F:'#ef4444',G:'#991b1b'};
var CUR=<%= cur_idx %>;
var TRAVAUX=<%= travaux.map{|t|{poste:t["poste"],cout_min:t["cout_min"].to_i,cout_max:t["cout_max"].to_i}}.to_json.html_safe %>;
var SIM_M=<%= [reste,0].max %>;

function clickSeg(idx){ if(idx<CUR){document.getElementById('dpe-slider').value=idx;slideTgt(idx);} }
function slideTgt(v){
  v=parseInt(v);
  if(v>=CUR){v=Math.max(0,CUR-1);document.getElementById('dpe-slider').value=v;}
  var pct=(v/7*100+1/14*100).toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('pin-tgt').style.left='calc('+pct+'% - 16px)';
  var dot=document.getElementById('pin-tgt-dot');
  dot.textContent=dpe_all[v]; dot.style.background=C_DPE[dpe_all[v]];
  document.querySelectorAll('.dpe-seg').forEach(function(seg,i){seg.style.opacity=(i>=v&&i<=CUR)?'1':'0.4';});
  calcTravaux(v);
}
function calcTravaux(tgtIdx){
  var sauts=CUR-tgtIdx; if(sauts<=0||!TRAVAUX.length)return;
  var mn=0,mx=0; TRAVAUX.slice(0,sauts).forEach(function(t){mn+=t.cout_min;mx+=t.cout_max;});
  var bv=document.getElementById('budget-val');
  if(bv) bv.textContent=mn.toLocaleString('fr-FR')+' – '+mx.toLocaleString('fr-FR')+' €';
}

var _p=<%= {"tres_modeste"=>0,"modeste"=>1,"intermediaire"=>2,"superieur"=>3}[@property.income_bracket.to_s] || 3 %>;
var _mT=[.70,.50,.35,.15];
function setProfil(p,k){
  _p=p;
  document.querySelectorAll('.sv-prof').forEach(function(b,i){b.classList.toggle('on',i===p);});
  calcAides();
}
function calcAides(){
  var mx=<%= budget_max %>;
  var mpr=Math.min(Math.round(mx*_mT[_p]),20000);
  var cee=Math.min(Math.round(mx*.08),4000);
  var reste=Math.max(0,mx-mpr-cee);
  var fmt=function(x){return x.toLocaleString('fr-FR')+' €';};
  var me=document.getElementById('mpr-val'); if(me) me.textContent=mpr>0?fmt(mpr):'—';
  var ce=document.getElementById('cee-val'); if(ce) ce.textContent=cee>0?fmt(cee):'—';
  var re=document.getElementById('reste-val'); if(re) re.textContent=reste>0?fmt(reste):mx>0?'0 €':'—';
  var fl=document.getElementById('fin-reste-lbl'); if(fl) fl.textContent=fmt(reste);
  SIM_M=reste; calcPret();
}

function calcPret(){
  var D=parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-d')?.value)||15;
  var T=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-t')?.value)||0;
  var dl=document.getElementById('sim-d-lbl'); if(dl) dl.textContent=D+' ans';
  var tl=document.getElementById('sim-t-lbl'); if(tl) tl.textContent=T.toFixed(1)+' %';
  var M=SIM_M,n=D*12,fmt=function(x){return Math.round(x).toLocaleString('fr-FR')+' €';};
  var ms=document.getElementById('sim-mens'),st=document.getElementById('sim-total'),sc=document.getElementById('sim-cout');
  if(!ms)return;
  if(T===0){ms.textContent=fmt(M/n)+'/mois';st.textContent=fmt(M);sc.textContent='0 €';}
  else{var r=(T/100)/12,m=M*r*Math.pow(1+r,n)/(Math.pow(1+r,n)-1),tot=m*n;ms.textContent=fmt(m)+'/mois';st.textContent=fmt(tot);sc.textContent=fmt(tot-M);}
}

function initShow(){
  var sl=document.getElementById('dpe-slider');
  if(sl) slideTgt(<%= tgt_idx %>);
  calcPret();
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initShow);
document.addEventListener('turbo:load', initShow);
</script>

          <script>
          function calcTravaux() {
            var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.travaux-cb');
            var min = 0, max = 0;
            var gains = [];
            cbs.forEach(function(cb) {
              if (cb.checked) {
                min += parseInt(cb.dataset.min) || 0;
                max += parseInt(cb.dataset.max) || 0;
                if (cb.dataset.gain) gains.push(cb.dataset.gain);
              }
            });
            var fmt = function(x){ return x.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'; };
            document.getElementById('travaux-montant').textContent = fmt(min) + ' – ' + fmt(max);
            document.getElementById('travaux-dpe-impact').textContent = gains.length > 0
              ? 'Impact : ' + gains.join(', ')
              : 'Aucun travaux sélectionné';
            var total = document.getElementById('travaux-total');
            total.style.background = gains.length > 0 ? '#047857' : '#6b7280';
            var simSlider = document.getElementById('sim-montant');
            if (simSlider && max > 0) { simSlider.value = max; if (typeof calcPret === 'function') calcPret(); }
            if (typeof calcAides === 'function') calcAides();
          }
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcTravaux);
          </script>
        <% end %>

          <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr; gap:0.75rem;" class="sim-results-grid">
            <div class="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl p-4 text-center">
              <p class="text-xs text-emerald-700 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide mb-1">Mensualité</p>
              <p class="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-800" id="sim-mensualite">—</p>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 text-center">
              <p class="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide mb-1">Coût total</p>
              <p class="text-2xl font-black text-gray-900" id="sim-cout-total">—</p>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-orange-50 border border-orange-200 rounded-xl p-4 text-center">
              <p class="text-xs text-orange-700 font-bold uppercase tracking-wide mb-1">Coût des intérêts</p>
              <p class="text-2xl font-black text-orange-800" id="sim-interets">—</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-3">💡 Simulation indicative hors assurance. Pour l'Éco-PTZ (taux 0%), réglez le curseur sur 0%.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <script>
    function calcPret() {
      var M = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-montant').value);
      var D = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-duree').value);
      var T = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-taux').value);
      document.getElementById('sim-montant-label').textContent = M.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €';
      document.getElementById('sim-duree-label').textContent = D + ' ans';
      document.getElementById('sim-taux-label').textContent = T.toFixed(1) + ' %';
      var n = D * 12;
      var fmt = function(x){ return Math.round(x).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'; };
      if (T === 0) {
        var mens = M / n;
        document.getElementById('sim-mensualite').textContent = fmt(mens) + '/mois';
        document.getElementById('sim-cout-total').textContent = fmt(M);
        document.getElementById('sim-interets').textContent = '0 €';
      } else {
        var r = (T / 100) / 12;
        var mens = M * r * Math.pow(1+r,n) / (Math.pow(1+r,n)-1);
        var total = mens * n;
        document.getElementById('sim-mensualite').textContent = fmt(mens) + '/mois';
        document.getElementById('sim-cout-total').textContent = fmt(total);
        document.getElementById('sim-interets').textContent = fmt(total - M);
      }
    }
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcPret);

var currentProfil = -1;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcPret);
var currentProfil = -1;
var mprTaux = [0.70, 0.50, 0.35, 0.15];
var mprLabels = ['Bleu - 70% max', 'Jaune - 50% max', 'Violet - 35% max', 'Rose - 15% max'];
function setProfil(p) {
  currentProfil = p;
  document.querySelectorAll('.profil-btn').forEach(function(b, i) {
    b.style.borderColor = i === p ? '#059669' : '';
    b.style.color = i === p ? '#047857' : '';
    b.style.background = i === p ? '#ecfdf5' : '';
  });
  calcAides();
}
function calcAides() {
  var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.travaux-cb');
  var maxBudget = 0;
  cbs.forEach(function(cb) { if (cb.checked) maxBudget += parseInt(cb.dataset.max) || 0; });
  var cee = maxBudget > 0 ? Math.min(Math.round(maxBudget * 0.08), 4000) : 0;
  document.getElementById('aide-cee').textContent = cee > 0 ? cee.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €' : '—';
  var mpr = 0;
  if (currentProfil >= 0 && maxBudget > 0) {
    mpr = Math.min(Math.round(maxBudget * mprTaux[currentProfil]), 20000);
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr').textContent = mpr.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €';
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr-label').textContent = mprLabels[currentProfil];
  } else {
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr-label').textContent = 'Sélectionnez votre profil';
  }
  var totalAides = (currentProfil >= 0 ? mpr : 0) + cee;
  var reste = Math.max(0, maxBudget - totalAides);
  document.getElementById('reste-financer').textContent = reste > 0 ? reste.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €' : maxBudget > 0 ? '0 €' : '—';
  var slider = document.getElementById('sim-montant');
  if (slider && reste > 0) { slider.value = reste; calcPret(); }
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { calcTravaux(); calcPret(); calcAides(); });
    </script>
    <% end %>

      <div class="flex items-start gap-3 sm:gap-4 px-4 sm:px-6 pt-4 sm:pt-6 pb-4 sm:pb-5 border-b-2 border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">

        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;background:white;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-charges" placeholder="ex : 400" min="0" value="0"
                 style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;"
                 oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">€/mois</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Apport disponible</label>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;background:white;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-apport" placeholder="ex : 5 000" min="0" value="0"
                 style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;"
                 oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">€</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Votre âge</label>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;background:white;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-age" placeholder="ex : 45" min="18" max="80"
                 style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;"
                 oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">ans</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div style="margin-bottom:1rem;">
      <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Situation professionnelle</label>
      <select id="sim-situation" onchange="calculerSimulation()"
              style="width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;font-size:0.875rem;background:white;">
        <option value="cdi">CDI / Fonctionnaire</option>
        <option value="independant">Indépendant / Professions libérales</option>
        <option value="retraite">Retraité</option>
      </select>
    </div>

    <%# Résultats %>
    <div id="sim-resultats" style="display:none;background:#f0fdf4;border:1px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:1rem;padding:1.5rem;margin-top:1rem;">
      <h4 style="font-size:0.875rem;font-weight:700;color:#166534;margin:0 0 1rem;">📊 Résultats de votre simulation</h4>
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:1rem;">
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Mensualité estimée</p>
          <p id="res-mensualite" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;color:#059669;margin:0;">—</p>
          <p style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#9ca3af;margin:4px 0 0;">dont éco-PTZ à 0%</p>
        </div>
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Taux d'endettement</p>
          <p id="res-endettement" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;margin:0;">—</p>
          <p id="res-endettement-label" style="font-size:0.65rem;margin:4px 0 0;">—</p>
        </div>
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Durée recommandée</p>
          <p id="res-duree" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;color:#1e40af;margin:0;">—</p>
        </div>
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Coût total du crédit</p>
          <p id="res-cout" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;color:#374151;margin:0;">—</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="res-alerte" style="display:none;margin-top:1rem;padding:12px;border-radius:0.75rem;font-size:0.8rem;"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    const RESTE = <%= reste_a_charge %>;
    const ECO_PTZ = <%= sim.eco_ptz_max_amount %>;

    function toggleSimulation() {
      const el = document.getElementById('simulation-bancaire');
      el.style.display = el.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
      if (el.style.display === 'block') el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }

    function formatEur(n) {
      return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'EUR', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(n);
    }

    function mensualite(capital, tauxAnnuel, mois) {
      if (tauxAnnuel === 0) return capital / mois;
      const t = tauxAnnuel / 12;
      return capital * t / (1 - Math.pow(1 + t, -mois));
    }

    function calculerSimulation() {
      const revenus    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-revenus').value) || 0;
      const charges    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-charges').value) || 0;
      const apport     = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-apport').value) || 0;
      const age        = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-age').value) || 0;
      const situation  = document.getElementById('sim-situation').value;

      if (revenus < 500 || age < 18) return;

      // Durée max selon âge (limite assurance 75 ans généralement)
      const dureeMax  = Math.min(Math.max(75 - age, 5), 20);
      const dureeReco = Math.min(dureeMax, 15);

      // Taux indicatif selon profil (approximation marché 2025)
      const taux = situation === 'cdi' ? 0.033 :
                   situation === 'independant' ? 0.037 : 0.038;

      // Capital à emprunter = reste à charge - apport - éco-PTZ
      const ecopty_eligible = Math.min(ECO_PTZ, RESTE);
      const capitalPret = Math.max(RESTE - apport - ecopty_eligible, 0);

      // Mensualités
      const mensEcoPtz  = mensualite(ecopty_eligible, 0, dureeReco * 12);
      const mensPret    = capitalPret > 0 ? mensualite(capitalPret, taux, dureeReco * 12) : 0;
      const mensTotal   = mensEcoPtz + mensPret;

      // Taux d'endettement
      const tauxEndet = ((charges + mensTotal) / revenus) * 100;

      // Coût total
      const coutTotal = mensPret * dureeReco * 12 - capitalPret;

      // Affichage
      document.getElementById('sim-resultats').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('res-mensualite').textContent = formatEur(mensTotal) + '/mois';
      document.getElementById('res-duree').textContent = dureeReco + ' ans';
      document.getElementById('res-cout').textContent = coutTotal > 0 ? formatEur(coutTotal) : '0 €';

      const endetEl = document.getElementById('res-endettement');
      const endetLabel = document.getElementById('res-endettement-label');
      endetEl.textContent = tauxEndet.toFixed(1) + '%';

      if (tauxEndet <= 33) {
        endetEl.style.color = '#059669';
        endetLabel.textContent = '✓ Dans les normes bancaires';
        endetLabel.style.color = '#059669';
      } else if (tauxEndet <= 40) {
        endetEl.style.color = '#d97706';
        endetLabel.textContent = '⚠ Limite acceptable';
        endetLabel.style.color = '#d97706';
      } else {
        endetEl.style.color = '#dc2626';
        endetLabel.textContent = '✗ Au-dessus des normes';
        endetLabel.style.color = '#dc2626';
      }

      const alerte = document.getElementById('res-alerte');
      if (tauxEndet > 40) {
        alerte.style.display = 'block';
        alerte.style.background = '#fef2f2';
        alerte.style.border = '1px solid #fecaca';
        alerte.style.color = '#991b1b';
        alerte.innerHTML = '⚠️ Votre taux d\'endettement dépasse 40%. Un courtier peut parfois trouver des solutions adaptées (modulation de durée, différé de remboursement). Pensez aussi à vérifier si votre éco-PTZ couvre une part plus grande des travaux.';
      } else if (capitalPret === 0) {
        alerte.style.display = 'block';
        alerte.style.background = '#f0fdf4';
        alerte.style.border = '1px solid #bbf7d0';
        alerte.style.color = '#166534';
        alerte.innerHTML = '🎉 Bonne nouvelle : l\'éco-PTZ à 0% et votre apport couvrent l\'intégralité du reste à charge. Aucun emprunt complémentaire nécessaire.';
      } else {
        alerte.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  </script>

  <% else %>
  <% end %>
<% end %><%# /owner-only: financement %>

</div>

<script>
function toggleMenu() {
  const menu = document.getElementById('mobile-menu');
  const b1 = document.getElementById('b1');
  const b2 = document.getElementById('b2');
  const b3 = document.getElementById('b3');
  const open = !menu.classList.contains('hidden');
  menu.classList.toggle('hidden');
  if (!open) {
    b1.style.transform = 'translateY(8px) rotate(45deg)';
    b2.style.opacity   = '0';
    b3.style.transform = 'translateY(-8px) rotate(-45deg)';
  } else {
    b1.style.transform = '';
    b2.style.opacity   = '1';
    b3.style.transform = '';
  }
}
</script>

# Files in the config/locales directory are used for internationalization and
# are automatically loaded by Rails. If you want to use locales other than
# English, add the necessary files in this directory.
#
# To use the locales, use `I18n.t`:
#
#     I18n.t "hello"
#
# In views, this is aliased to just `t`:
#
#     <%= t("hello") %>
#
# To use a different locale, set it with `I18n.locale`:
#
#     I18n.locale = :es
#
# This would use the information in config/locales/es.yml.
#
# To learn more about the API, please read the Rails Internationalization guide
# at https://guides.rubyonrails.org/i18n.html.
#
# Be aware that YAML interprets the following case-insensitive strings as
# booleans: `true`, `false`, `on`, `off`, `yes`, `no`. Therefore, these strings
# must be quoted to be interpreted as strings. For example:
#
#     en:
#       "yes": yup
#       enabled: "ON"

===== config/routes.rb =====
Rails.application.routes.draw do
  get "pages/home"
  devise_for :users

  root "pages#home"
  get "/a-propos", to: "pages#about", as: :about
  get "/borne-electrique",    to: "pages#borne_electrique",   as: :borne_electrique
  get "/panneaux-solaires",   to: "pages#panneaux_solaires",  as: :panneaux_solaires
  get "/location-meublee",    to: "pages#location_meublee",   as: :location_meublee
  get "/amenagement-combles", to: "pages#amenagement_combles", as: :amenagement_combles
  get "/espace-exterieur",    to: "pages#espace_exterieur",   as: :espace_exterieur
  get "/box-stockage",        to: "pages#box_stockage",        as: :box_stockage

  get "/mentions-legales",    to: "pages#mentions_legales",   as: :mentions_legales
  get "/confidentialite",     to: "pages#confidentialite",    as: :confidentialite
  get "/contact",             to: "pages#contact",            as: :contact

  resources :properties do
    member do
      post :analyze
      post :publish
      post :unpublish
      get  :preview
      patch :update_dpe_target
      patch :update_income_bracket
    end
    resources :documents, only: [:new, :create, :destroy]
    resources :offers, only: [:new, :create]
  end

  resources :offers, only: [:index, :update]
  namespace :admin do
    resources :local_aid_schemes do
      member { post :toggle_active }
    end
  end

end
